# Initialization Event

country_event = {
	id = turkish_debug.1
	title = turkish_debug.1.t
	desc = turkish_debug.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_TUR_attack_PKK
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
		change_ruling_party_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = turkish_debug.1.d1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_debug.1.a executed"
		country_event = turkish_debug.2
	}
}

# Game-Start Economic Events (Black Wednesday)

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.2 # The Rates Collapse
	title = turkish_events.2.t
	desc = turkish_events.2.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.2.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 200.00 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		newline = yes
		add_stability = 0.05
	}

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.2.b executed"
		add_ideas = TUR_honeymoon
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.3 # Five Plus Five
	title = turkish_events.3.t
	desc = turkish_events.3.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.3.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.3.a executed"
		random_list = { # Keeping Suleyman for 3 more years will delay the rise of AKP and potentially prevent black wednesday
			20 = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_3_more_years_TT
			}
			80 = {
				country_event = {
					id = turkish_events.4
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
		newline = yes
		add_stability = -0.05
		newline	= yes
		increase_corruption = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.3.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.3.b executed"
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
			id = turkish_events.4
			days = 3
		}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.15
	title = turkish_events.15.t
	desc = turkish_events.15.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.15.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.15.a executed"
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = TUR_disinflation_gamble
			add_idea = TUR_black_wednesday
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					TUR_lira_stability > 6
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_we_prevented_disaster_TT
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = -0.25 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = -0.25 }
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
			newline = yes
			add_stability = -0.10
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.16
	title = turkish_events.16.t
	desc = turkish_events.16.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.16.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.16.a executed"
		TUR = {
			country_event = turkish_events.17
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = TUR }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = TUR
					value > 19
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.16.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.16.b executed"
		TUR = {
			country_event = turkish_events.18
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

# Greco-Turkish War Chain
country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.20
	title = turkish_events.20.t
	desc = turkish_events.20.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.20.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.20.a executed"
		add_manpower = -2
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					country_event = {
						id = turkish_events.21
						days = 1
					}
				}
				50 = {
					country_event = {
						id = turkish_events.22
						days = 1
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							TUR = {
								has_idea = NATO_member
							}
							GRE = {
								has_idea = NATO_member
							}
						}
						NATO_leave = yes
						set_country_flag = TUR_banned_from_NATO
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.21
	title = turkish_events.21.t
	desc = turkish_events.21.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.21.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.21.a executed"
		activate_mission = TUR_dash_across_the_strait
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					GRE = {
						has_idea = NATO_member
					}
					TUR = {
						has_idea = NATO_member
					}
				}
				remove_ideas = NATO_member
				remove_from_tech_sharing_group = NATO_Tech_Share
				# If we are the faction leader, transfer leadership before leaving
				if = {
					limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					# Find another NATO member to transfer leadership to
					random_other_country = {
						limit = {
							has_idea = NATO_member
							is_in_faction_with = ROOT
						}
						# Transfer faction leadership
						set_faction_leader = yes
					}
				}
				leave_faction = yes
				GRE = {
					declare_war_on = {
						target = TUR
						type = liberate_wargoal
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = turkish_events.23
	title = turkish_events.23.t
	desc = turkish_events.23.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = turkish_events.23.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:turkish_events.23.a executed"
		hidden_effect = {
			669 = { transfer_state_to = CRM }
			CRM = {
				division_template = {
					name = "Qırım Leşkeri"
					priority = 0
					regiments = {
						Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						Arty_Bat = { x = 0 y = 3 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 1 y = 2 }
						Arty_Bat = { x = 1 y = 3 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 2 y = 0 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 2 y = 1 }
						Militia_Bat = { x = 2 y = 2 }
						Arty_Bat = { x = 2 y = 3 }
					}
					support = {
						L_Recce_Comp = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						L_Engi_Comp = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						combat_service_support_company = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						Arty_Battery = { x = 0 y = 3 }
					}
				}
				669 = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"1st. Qırım Leşkeri\" division_template = \"Qırım Leşkeri\" start_experience_factor = 1.0 start_equipment_factor = 0.8"
						owner = CRM
						prioritize_location = 9680
					}
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"2nd. Qırım Şimşekleri\" division_template = \"Qırım Leşkeri\" start_experience_factor = 1.0 start_equipment_factor = 0.8"
						owner = CRM
						prioritize_location = 14328

country_event = {
	id = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1
	title = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.name
	desc = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_sabra

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# M60T Sabra
	option = {
		name = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.a"

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.10 days = 120 }
	}

	# M60-84
	option = {
		name = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.c"

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.12 days = 120 }
	}

	# M60-2000
	option = {
		name = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -40 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.11 days = 120 }
	}
}

# M60T Sabra
country_event = {
	id = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.10
	title = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.10.name
	desc = tur_m60t_upgrade_tender.10.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_sabra

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "M60T Sabra"
			type = medium_tank_chassis_0
			parent_version = 0
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = tank_medium_cannon

				special_type_slot_2 = additional_mg
				special_type_slot_3 = reactive_armor_gen1

			}
			upgrades = {
				tank_nsb_armor_upgrade = 2
				tank_nsb_engine_upgrade = 2
			}

			model = "TUR_MD_medium_armor_entity"

				}
			}
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = medium_tank_chassis_0
					version_name = "M60T Sabra"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				efficiency = 20
			}

###############################################################
# TANK 2000 TENDER
# (the Germany refusal keeps its 1 day timing; the choice is made
#  at the start and the tanks arrive in the stockpile when the
#  decision finishes, 100 days after it was taken. The decision's
#  remove_effect fires the matching delivery event, 10-13)
###############################################################

country_event = {
	id = tur_tank_2000_tender.1
	title = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.name
	desc = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_mbt_2000_2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Leopard 2A6 (Germany refuses)
	option = {
		name = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tank_2000_tender.1.a"

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_germany_refused }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_germany_refused
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = tur_tank_2000_tender.2 days = 7 }
		}
	}

	# Leclerc
	option = {
		name = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tank_2000_tender.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_leclerc

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -80 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

	# Abrams
	option = {
		name = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tank_2000_tender.1.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_abrams

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -80 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

	# T-84 Yatagan
	option = {
		name = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.d
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tank_2000_tender.1.d"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_t84

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -50 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}

	# Secondhand Leopard 2A4 (only after Germany refused)
	option = {
		name = tur_tank_2000_tender.1.e
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_tank_2000_tender.1.e"

		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_germany_refused
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -30 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_done
		set_country_flag = TUR_tank_2000_leopard_2a4
	}
}

					armor_type_slot = tank_steel_armor_gen2

					reload_type_slot = manual_loading
					special_type_slot_1 = smoke_launchers_3

					special_type_slot_4 = tank_battlestation_2_thermal

				model = "TUR_armor_Bat_3_entity"
			}
		}

		# 8. L44 / Basic / Cheap
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_gun_l44
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_armor_basic
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_engine_cheap
			}
			create_equipment_variant = {
				name = "Altay"
				type = medium_tank_chassis_2
				parent_version = 0
				allow_without_tech = yes
				modules = {
					main_armament_slot = tank_medium_cannon_2
					secondary_armament_slot = afv_coax_machine_gun_3
					ammunition_load_slot = anti_armor_main_ammo_2
					turret_type_slot = tank_base_tank_turret
					suspension_type_slot = tank_torsion_bar_suspension_medium
					armor_type_slot = tank_steel_armor_gen2
					engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen3
					reload_type_slot = manual_loading
					special_type_slot_1 = smoke_launchers_3
					special_type_slot_2 = additional_mg
					special_type_slot_4 = tank_battlestation_2_thermal
					special_type_slot_6 = spaced_armor_gen2
				}
				upgrades = { tank_nsb_armor_upgrade = 2 }
				icon = "GFX_TUR_LAND_Altay1A1"
				model = "TUR_armor_Bat_3_entity"
			}
		}

		# Production
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_otokar
				159 = { #Ankara
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = medium_tank_chassis_2 
					version_name = "Altay"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				efficiency = 20
				industrial_manufacturer = mio:TUR_otokar_tank_manufacturer
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_altay_bmc
				159 = { #Ankara
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = medium_tank_chassis_2 
					version_name = "Altay"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				efficiency = 40
				#industrial_manufacturer = mio:TUR_bmc_tank_manufacturer
			}
		}

		# Cleanup
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_gun_l55
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_gun_l44
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_armor_composite
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_armor_basic
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_engine_strong
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_engine_cheap
		clr_country_flag = TUR_altay_bmc_delayed
		set_country_flag = TUR_altay_program_done
	}
}

	}
}

	picture = GFX_TUR_yeni_altay

	is_triggered_only = yes

			base = 10
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_yeni_altay_started
		set_country_flag = TUR_yeni_altay_korean

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Altay T1 will be delivered at 180 days
		country_event = { id = tur_yeni_altay_development.2 days = 180 }
	}

	# Domestic BMC Batu Engine Path
	option = {
		name = tur_yeni_altay_development.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_yeni_altay_development.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_yeni_altay_started
		set_country_flag = TUR_yeni_altay_batu

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Altay T2 (with Batu engine) will be delivered at exactly 360 days
		country_event = { id = tur_yeni_altay_development.3 days = 360 }
	}
}

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.2 }
	}

	# MRAP choice
	option = {
		name = tur_mrap_tender.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap_tender.1.b"

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_mrap_done }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.3 }
	}
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. LIGHT TRANSPORT: BMC 235 or BMC Efe
#    (choice is instant, delivery comes 100 days later)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_mrap_tender.2
	title = tur_mrap_tender.2.name
	desc = tur_mrap_tender.2.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_mrap_2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# BMC 235
	option = {
		name = tur_mrap_tender.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap_tender.2.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.10 days = 100 }

		set_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.4 } }
	}

	# BMC Efe
	option = {
		name = tur_mrap_tender.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap_tender.2.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.11 days = 100 }

		set_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.4 } }
	}
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# HIDDEN ROUTER
# ------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_mrap_tender.4
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done
				has_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_mrap_done
			}
			set_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_light_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_mrap_done
			clr_country_flag = TUR_mrap_tender_started
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = tur_mrap_tender.1 days = 10 }
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_mrap_done }
		}

			NOT = { has_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_utility_done }

	title = tur_mrap3_tender.2.name
	desc = tur_mrap3_tender.2.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_mrap_8

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# BMC Vuran
	option = {
		name = tur_mrap3_tender.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap3_tender.2.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -12 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap3_tender.10 days = 100 }

		set_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_mrap_done
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = tur_mrap3_tender.4 } }
	}

	# BMC Amazon

		name = tur_mrap3_tender.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap3_tender.2.b"

			base = 10
		}

		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap3_tender.11 days = 100 }

		set_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_mrap_done
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = tur_mrap3_tender.4 } }
	}

	# Katmerciler Ates
	option = {
		name = tur_mrap3_tender.2.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap3_tender.2.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -8 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_mrap3_tender.12 days = 100 }

		set_country_flag = TUR_mrap3_tender_mrap_done
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = tur_mrap3_tender.4 } }
	}

		name = tur_mrap3_tender.2.d
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_mrap3_tender.2.d"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

country_event = {
	id = tur_spaa_tender.1
	title = tur_spaa_tender.1.name
	desc = tur_spaa_tender.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_spaa

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Atılgan and Zıpkın
	option = {
		name = tur_spaa_tender.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_spaa_tender.1.a"

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_spaa_tender.10 days = 100 }
	}

	# ACV Roland
	option = {
		name = tur_spaa_tender.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_spaa_tender.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_spaa_tender.11 days = 100 }
	}
}

	title = tur_spaa_tender.10.name
	desc = tur_spaa_tender.10.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_spaa

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "KMS Zıpkın"
			type = medium_tank_aa_chassis_1
			parent_version = 1
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = missile_launcher_system
				reload_type_slot = four_missile_pack
				ammunition_load_slot = spaa_short_range_missiles_2

				suspension_type_slot = tank_wheeled_suspension_light
				engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen2

			}

			}
		}

			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = missile_launcher_system
				reload_type_slot = eight_missile_pack

				efficiency = 20
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = TUR_burc
				num_of_military_factories > 0
			}
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = medium_tank_aa_chassis_3
					version_name = "Burç"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				progress = 0.1
				efficiency = 20
			}
		}

		# Cleanup
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_cannon_heavy
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_cannon_light
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_chassis_armored
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_chassis_mobile
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_missile_long
		clr_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_missile_std
		set_country_flag = TUR_gurz_burc_program_done
	}
}

	id = turkey_afv_tender.1
	title = turkey_afv_tender.1.name
	desc = turkey_afv_tender.1.desc
	picture = GFX_turkey_afv_tender

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Otokar Arma
	option = {
		name = turkey_afv_tender.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event turkey_afv_tender.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_afv_tender_arma

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Design is added after 120 days
		country_event = { id = turkey_afv_tender.2 days = 120 }
	}

	# FNSS Pars III
	option = {
		name = turkey_afv_tender.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event turkey_afv_tender.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_afv_tender_pars3

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		# Design is added after 120 days
		country_event = { id = turkey_afv_tender.2 days = 120 }
	}
}

		}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Pars Alpha ZMA 120 HT / Otokar Arma XC-8 120mm / BMC Altug Hitfact 120mm
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_ynzma_leg2.1
	title = tur_ynzma_leg2.1.name
	desc = tur_ynzma_leg2.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_lt_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Pars Alpha ZMA 120 HT
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg2.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg2.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma2_alpha

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg2.2 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Arma XC-8 120mm
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg2.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg2.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma2_arma

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg2.2 days = 30 }
	}

	# BMC Altug Hitfact 120mm
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg2.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg2.1.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 2 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma2_altug

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -15 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg2.2 days = 30 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Pars Alpha ZMA / Otokar Arma 2 / BMC Altug
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_ynzma_leg3.1
	title = tur_ynzma_leg3.1.name
	desc = tur_ynzma_leg3.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_apc_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Pars Alpha ZPT
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg3.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg3.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_alpha

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg3.2 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Arma 2 ZPT
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg3.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg3.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_arma2

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -12 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg3.2 days = 30 }
	}

	# BMC Altug ZPT
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg3.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg3.1.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 2 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_altug

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -8 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg3.2 days = 30 }
	}
}

		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma3_turret_unmanned

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg3.4 days = 30 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Kaplan IFV / Otokar Tulpar IFV
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_ynzma_leg4.1
	title = tur_ynzma_leg4.1.name
	desc = tur_ynzma_leg4.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_ifv_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Kaplan IFV
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg4.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg4.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma4_kaplan

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg4.2 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Tulpar IFV
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg4.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg4.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma4_tulpar

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -8 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg4.2 days = 30 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
# DAY 0 - Choose vehicle: FNSS Kaplan LT / Otokar Tulpar LT
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
country_event = {
	id = tur_ynzma_leg5.1
	title = tur_ynzma_leg5.1.name
	desc = tur_ynzma_leg5.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_ynzma_tlt_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# FNSS Kaplan LT
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg5.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg5.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma5_kaplan

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -6 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg5.2 days = 30 }
	}

	# Otokar Tulpar LT
	option = {
		name = tur_ynzma_leg5.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_ynzma_leg5.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_ynzma5_tulpar

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_ynzma_leg5.2 days = 30 }
	}
}

###############################################################
# SPG TENDER
# (choice is instant at day 0, delivery comes 120 days later)
###############################################################

country_event = {
	id = tur_spg_tender.1
	title = tur_spg_tender.1.name
	desc = tur_spg_tender.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_spg

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Korean K9 Thunder (license production, becomes T-155 Firtina)
	option = {
		name = tur_spg_tender.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_spg_tender.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -30 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_spg_tender.10 days = 120 }
	}

	# German PzH 2000 (very capable, very expensive)
	option = {
		name = tur_spg_tender.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_spg_tender.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_stability < 0.10 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -60 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_spg_tender.11 days = 120 }
	}

	# British AS-90 (cheaper off-the-shelf purchase, no license)
	option = {
		name = tur_spg_tender.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_spg_tender.1.c"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -35 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_spg_tender.12 days = 120 }
	}
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# DELIVERY EVENTS (fire 120 days after the choice is made)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# K9 Thunder -> T-155 Firtina (license production begins)
country_event = {
	id = tur_spg_tender.10
	title = tur_spg_tender.10.name
	desc = tur_spg_tender.10.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_firtina

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = TUR_spg_firtina

		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "T-155 Firtina"
			type = medium_tank_artillery_chassis_2
			parent_version = 1
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = art_med_gun_gen2
			   reload_type_slot = automatic_loading
			   ammunition_load_slot = artillery_medium_ammo_2
			   turret_type_slot = tank_base_tank_turret
				suspension_type_slot = tank_pneumatic_suspension_medium
				engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen3
				special_type_slot_1 = empty
				special_type_slot_2 = fire_ammo_2
				special_type_slot_3 = fire_ammo_2
				special_type_slot_4 = tank_battlestation_3
				special_type_slot_5 = multiple_rounds_medium_gen1
			}
			upgrades = {
				tank_nsb_fire_upgrade = 2
			}
			icon = "GFX_TUR_LAND_T_155_A1"
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				159 = { #Ankara
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
			add_equipment_production = {
				equipment = {
					type = medium_tank_artillery_chassis_2
					version_name = "T-155 Firtina"
				}
				requested_factories = 1
				efficiency = 20
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = tur_spg_tender.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_spg_tender.10.a"
	}
}

###############################################################
# HOWITZER DEVELOPMENT: ARPAN vs YAVUZ
# (choice is instant at day 0, design arrives 120 days later)
###############################################################

country_event = {
	id = tur_howitzer_dev.1
	title = tur_howitzer_dev.1.name
	desc = tur_howitzer_dev.1.desc
	picture = GFX_TUR_arpan

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# Arpan
	option = {
		name = tur_howitzer_dev.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_howitzer_dev.1.a"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_howitzer_arpan

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_howitzer_dev.10 days = 120 }
	}

	# Yavuz
	option = {
		name = tur_howitzer_dev.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Event tur_howitzer_dev.1.b"

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
		}

		set_country_flag = TUR_howitzer_yavuz

		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -25 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		country_event = { id = tur_howitzer_dev.11 days = 120 }
	}
}
